- The interface language does not go into an AI call: the tutor speaks in the module's explanation language (concepts 10 spec; R92 spec).

**The device's language** is for the screens where there is no learner yet or where an adult reads: sign-in, the choice of the learner, the start of work, the failure screens and the section for adults.
- The adult's choice on this device is the language buttons on the sign-in screen and in the section for adults. Each language is named by its own name — "English", «Русский» — and with its own `lang`. These are buttons, not a list `<select>`, which would change the screen on a choice (WCAG 3.2.2; section 3.9). A tap changes the screen's language at once, without a reload.
- Without a choice, the first language of `navigator.languages` that the lookup of RFC 4647, "Lookup", finds: the tag is truncated from the right, subtag by subtag — `ru-RU` → `ru` —, until a language of the build is found, and a single-letter subtag goes together with the next one, as in `canvas`. There is no "best match", as with the CLDR matcher: in MathTrail it gave Kazakh and Belarusian to Russian (sources 8.1). If none is found, English.
- The choice lies in the device settings — a small IndexedDB database of its own (section 3.2; the 2026-11-02 amendment to R04). Signing out and the sign-in of another account do not erase it: the sign-in screen is seen before the account. "Erase everything on this device" and the browser itself erase it. The account has no language of its own.
- `app` draws the first screen once it has read the device settings: the database is small, and the language must not change under the finger. If the database did not open, `navigator.languages`. The screen "in a frame" (section 3.7) opens no storage and takes `navigator.languages`. The other tabs of `app` learn of a change of the device's language through `BroadcastChannel`.
- The built `index.html` has `<html lang="en">` and `<noscript>` in English and Russian, and the Russian paragraph has `lang="ru"`: before the script nobody knows the language (the 2026-11-02 amendment to R09).
- Google's sign-in window is in the screen's language: before the sign-in `app` sets `languageCode` of Firebase Auth, and it goes into the sign-in window if the provider can do that (sources 8.1). That it does come out so on devices is checked by the first task of the sign-in code together with Д8 in table 4 of RUN.md.

**Texts from `api`.** Legal texts and consent are pages of `app` (architecture 3.13 spec), and in a self-hosting copy they are the texts of its operator, which the copy's `api` serves (R115 spec; П-24 closed). The texts are in English only, and the English text is the one in force: so the author decided, not by the recommendation of `app` — a text that is accepted has no translations that could diverge (privacy 7 spec; the 2026-11-10 amendment to R10). On a screen in another language, above the text there is a line of `app` in the screen's language saying that the text is in English, and the text itself has `lang="en"`. The gist of the consent — who the operator is, which data and why, to whom they go, how to withdraw it — `app` shows briefly and in the screen's language beside the English text (R116 spec). `app` gets the texts by the method `LegalService.GetLegalTexts` — Connect GET, without a token: the policy is read before sign-in too. It names the screen's language by the field `interface_language`, not by the header `Accept-Language`: the language of the section for adults is not necessarily the browser's language. The body of the text comes in English, Markdown without HTML, pictures and links, and the gist in the request's language, if the texts' manifest carries it, otherwise in English. Its language is named by the field `summary_language`, and by it `app` sets its `lang` (R130 spec; [the contracts text](../spec/contracts/README.md), section 15). What else the part `legal/` brings is T14. Since v0.15.0 of `spec` a version may have a translation into an interface language — the fields `translation` and `translation_language` —, and on a screen in that language `app` shows it below the line saying the English is in force, and before the English text, with its own `lang` and the mark "for reading only": the translation has no force of its own, and a consent names the version, not the translation (R141 spec; the 2026-11-11 amendment to R10). The consent request names the version the person saw; a version that is no longer current gives `CAUSE_TEXT_NOT_CURRENT`, and `app` reads the texts anew and asks for consent again (R131 spec; Д190 in table 4 of RUN.md of `spec`). **Code** — since T32 (R27): `src/legal/texts.ts` is `GetLegalTexts` by the open transport past the HTTP cache, with the screen's language and with `unimplemented` of an older `api` as "no text in force", `body.ts` and `Markdown.tsx` are the body by the lexer of `marked` as React elements, without a string of HTML, and HTML, a link and a picture as their own text, `LegalText.tsx` is a text as a disclosure in place; the invite code screen shows `privacy-policy` and `terms-of-use` and asks for the tick, and `Session.createAccount` names their versions; the tick holds to the versions shown. Checked in Chromium: the sign-in path with the texts in `en`, a screen in `ru` with the translation and the `lang` of each part, a failure to read and "Try again", `CAUSE_TEXT_NOT_CURRENT` with the tick cleared and the focus on it, and axe on a screen with the texts open.

**Two languages on a screen.**
- `<html lang>` is the screen's language: on the learner's screens, the language of their profile, on the others, the device's language (WCAG 3.1.1).
- Each block of the module's text and of the tutor's words has its own `lang`, the module's explanation language (3.1.2): "Screen readers can load the correct pronunciation rules" (sources 8.1). The name of a module, a lesson or a skill inside a string of `app` is a placeholder element with its own `lang`, not a piece of the string.
- `app` sets the canvas element's `lang` to the screen's language: the buttons' captions are in it, and the element itself sets the manifest's language on the statement, the workbook's lines and the parts of the example (R36 spec; R32 canvas). The number of a part of the example is in the element's language, and the part itself in the manifest's language (R42 canvas).
- The module's card in "My subjects" and in the section for adults names the explanation language if it is not the screen's language — by the name from `Intl.DisplayNames`, and in Russian with a lower-case letter, as a language's name stands inside a phrase: JavaScriptCore writes it with a capital, «Английский», and V8 and SpiderMonkey, «английский» (sources 8.2; measurements 2). The names of anchor skills are English (R50 spec), with `lang="en"`, and for now they are not translated: a translation would touch R50 spec and the format (R91 spec).
- Voicing is in the language of each text, in its own voice (section 3.5).

**Translations.**
- A dictionary per language is a TypeScript module: `src/i18n/en.ts` and `src/i18n/ru.ts`. The keys are dotted, the first word being the screen: `task.check`, `device.erase-all`. English is the source, with `as const`. A language's dictionary type is derived from it: a string key has a string with the same placeholders, which the type finds by the pattern `{name}`, and a plural form has an object of that language's categories, for Russian `one`, `few`, `many` and `other`. The Russian dictionary `satisfies` this type: `tsc` lets through no missing key, no extra key, no placeholder with another name and no missing category, so a pull request without a Russian string does not pass `ci-lint` (section 3.1). A note to the translator is a JSDoc comment on the key.
- Placeholders are by name, `{count}`, `{module}`; there is no concatenation of strings: languages have different word orders. A placeholder that needs its own `lang` is an element, not a string.
- Plural forms are an explicit marker with the categories of CLDR. English has two — `one` and `other` —, and Russian four — `one`, `few`, `many` and `other` —, and the integer 0 is `many`, while `other` is only fractions (sources 8.1, 8.2). `Intl.PluralRules` chooses the category. `other` is always there, and if the browser returned a category the dictionary does not have, `other` is taken.
- The numbers, sizes and dates that `app` writes are `Intl.NumberFormat` and `Intl.DateTimeFormat` with `[language, "en"]`, the names of languages are `Intl.DisplayNames`, the sorting of profiles and modules is `Intl.Collator`. `Intl` does not touch the module's numbers and what the learner might copy into an answer: in Russian it writes a decimal comma, and the format reads `1,5` as two numbers ([the format text](../spec/format/README.md), sections 15 and 20).
- Codes from the server — the verdict, the reason of "unreadable" (section 3.3), the choice's reason code, the outcome of the own key — `app` translates by a typed choice. The choice's reason code is the closed `ReasonCode`, nine values with `REASON_CODE_ROTATION`, "in rotation", without parameters: each has words for the adult in progress, at the attempt, in `en` and `ru`, and the code is not shown to the learner (R100, R107 spec; Д106 and Д114 in table 4 of RUN.md of `spec`). A code the build does not know gets a generic string, not an error: a newer `api` may send a new code (section 3.7). Since T44 the words of the codes are `CHOICE_REASON_KEYS` in `src/adults/progress.ts`, and only there: no file of the learner's screens knows them or the field `reason_code`, and a test holds this (R35).
- The pseudo-language — English, made one and a half times longer and with diacritics — the tests build from the English dictionary themselves, and it does not go into the build.

**Rules of strings.**
- A string does not give away the learner's gender, nor do the tutor's words (R70 spec; concepts 10 spec). In Russian, without the past tense and short adjectives about the learner: «Здесь всё верно», not «Ты верно нашёл». The mode «сам», "self", is «Самостоятельно» on its button. In the section for adults the same is written about a pseudonym: «Урок пройден: Маша», not «Маша прошла урок».
- On the learner's screens, in Russian, the learner is not addressed: on the buttons and in the step's lines, the infinitive, «Проверить», «Отметить, что известно и что найти», and elsewhere impersonal phrases, «Нужна помощь взрослого». «Ты» to an adult learner and «вы» to a child are equally awkward, and the infinitive suits everyone. In English "you" does not give away gender and will do.
- In the section for adults, in Russian, «вы».
- The pseudonym and the names of a module, a lesson and a skill stand where they are not declined: «Профиль: Маша», not «профиль Маши».
- AI does not speak of itself in the first person: «Прочитано: 6», not «Я прочитал 6», as the examples of `spec` write — that is the masculine gender and a person where AI reads (П-15, closed by R123 spec; R91 spec).
- The voicing button is "Read aloud", «Озвучить», not "listen", «прослушать»: concepts 8 spec keeps that word for an action of the text canvas.
- Strings are short: a child of 6 reads them too. They have no external links (privacy 1, 7 spec).
- The screen's words may differ from the terms of the vocabulary of concepts 15 spec: the documents keep the terms, and the screen keeps the words of the glossary, and one is not corrected by the other.

**Glossary** — the words that all screens take the same way. The code writes the full phrases by the rules above, and the author reads them in the pull request.

| What | `en` | `ru` | Where |
|---|---|---|---|
| send the work to be read | Check | «Проверить» | the task screen (section 3.3) |
| hint | Hint | «Подсказка» | the task screen |
| nothing to hint | This task has nothing more to hint | «У этой задачи больше нечего подсказать» | the block "Hint" (R22) |
| explain differently | Explain differently | «Объяснить иначе» | the block "Tutor": the infinitive, the learner is not addressed |
| no other words | The tutor has no other words for this | «Других слов у наставника для этого нет» | after "Explain differently" |
| show the solution | Show the solution; Once the solution is shown, the task is over; Show it | «Показать решение»; «Когда решение показано, задача заканчивается»; «Показать» | the task screen (R22) |
| solution shown | Solution shown | «Решение показано» | the attempt's outcome |
| the hint ladder not gone through | The solution can be shown after a few more answers | «Решение можно показать после ещё нескольких ответов» | the refusal to show it to a child |
| step done | Step done | «Шаг готов» | the panel of steps |
| the mode "by steps" | Step by step | «По шагам» | the task screen |
| the mode "self" | On my own | «Самостоятельно» | the task screen |
| the verdict "correct" | Correct | «Верно» | after the check |
| the verdict "not yet" | Not yet | «Пока нет» | after the check (product 7 spec) |
| the work unreadable | Can't read this | «Не получается прочитать» | after the check (R56 spec) |
| what was read | Read: {text} | «Прочитано: {text}» | after the check |
| an attempt without a check | Not checked | «Без проверки» | after the check (R63 spec) |
| to the lesson's next segment | Continue | «Продолжить» | the lesson (section 3.4) |

| the kinds of segment | Explanation; Worked example; Task | «Объяснение»; «Пример с разбором»; «Задача» | the strip of segments and the segment's heading |
| where the segment is | {kind} · {n} of {total} | «{kind} · {n} из {total}» | the segment's heading |
| how far the segment is | passed; now; later | «пройдено»; «сейчас»; «дальше» | the chip's description: «звено» is neuter, not «пройден» as for a step |
| the lesson's task is over | This task is over | «Эта задача закончилась» | a chip that does not open |

| a chip that does not open is pressed | {n} {kind}: not open yet; {n} {kind}: already over | «{n} {kind}: ещё не открыто»; «{n} {kind}: уже закончилась» | a line of the lesson screen: it names the chip, not the segment shown |
| leave the lesson | Leave the lesson | «Выйти из урока» | the lesson |
| begin the task again | Begin the task again | «Начать задачу заново» | a task of the lesson, abandoned or not begun |
| a task is left in the lesson | The lesson has a task to finish | «В уроке осталась задача» | after the refusal of "lesson passed" |
| the lesson no longer suits | This lesson doesn't suit this profile any more | «Этот урок больше не подходит этому профилю» | the end of the lesson, a neutral phrase (R04 spec) |
| the lesson abandoned | The lesson wasn't opened for 30 days | «Урок не открывали 30 дней» | the end of the lesson, a neutral phrase |
| the lesson ended | This lesson is over | «Этот урок закончился» | the end of the lesson for a reason the build does not know |
| a lesson begun | A lesson in progress; Continue the lesson | «Начатый урок»; «Продолжить урок» | "My subjects" |
| what next | What's next | «Что дальше» | the task's outcome, the end of the lesson, "My subjects" (R24) |
| the next lesson | Next lesson | «Следующий урок» | the task's outcome, the end of the lesson |
| the next task | Next task | «Следующая задача» | the task's outcome, the end of the lesson |
| nothing to offer | There is nothing more to offer for now | «Пока больше нечего предложить» | the engine choice "nothing to give" |
| something else | Something else; The task in progress closes, and it can't be gone back to; Go on | «Что-то другое»; «Начатая задача закроется, и к ней не вернуться»; «Перейти» | "My subjects" beside "Continue the task" |
| a lesson of the module | A lesson: {module} | «Урок: {module}» | "My subjects", when the lesson's name is not on the device: the module is not declined |

| the text will not sound | This device has no voice for {language}; On this device, an adult turns voicing on first | «На этом устройстве нет голоса для языка: {language}»; «На этом устройстве озвучку сначала включает взрослый» | a line by the button "Read aloud" and under the button "Voicing": the language by its name from `Intl.DisplayNames`, in lower case, inside the phrase (R36) |
| the learner's button | Voicing | «Озвучка» | at the top of the task screen, `aria-pressed` (R36) |
| the name of a tutor's mark | Tutor's mark {n} of {total} | «Пометка наставника {n} из {total}» | the screen reader, below |

| the section for adults | For grown-ups | «Для взрослых» | the choice of the learner, the header |
| close the section | Close the section | «Закрыть раздел» | the header of the section for adults (R29) |
| back to the section | Back to the section | «Назад в раздел» | the section's header, but for the first page |
| profiles | Profiles | «Профили» | the section's first page |
| the profile page | Profile: {pseudonym} | «Профиль: {pseudonym}» | the section's first page and the heading of the profile page: the pseudonym is not declined |

| the default mode | Default mode; Each task begins in it. The learner can switch it on the task screen | «Режим по умолчанию»; «С него начинается каждая задача. Ученик может переключить его на экране задачи» | the profile page (R05) |
| the language of the learner's screens | Language of the learner's screens | «Язык экранов ученика» | the profile page (R10) |

| the language not in the build | The profile's language is {language}, which this version of the app doesn't have. | voicing | Voicing; Read the tutor's words aloud; Speed of voicing; Normal; Slow | «Озвучка»; «Озвучивать слова наставника»; «Скорость озвучки»; «Обычная»; «Медленная» | the profile page (R07) |
| the device's voice | The tutor's words are read by a voice of the device. Each device's voice is chosen on its page This device | «Слова наставника читает голос устройства. Голос каждого устройства выбирают на его странице «Это устройство»» | the profile page (R36) |
| voicing and a screen reader | If you listen to a screen reader, turn voicing off: the two would speak at once | «Если вы слушаете программу чтения с экрана, озвучку лучше выключить: они говорили бы разом» | the profile page (section 3.9) |

Нужно попробовать снова» | "This device", the section "Voicing" (R36): the sample is not "listen", «прослушать», but "Read aloud", «Озвучить», as on the button |
| how to add a voice | A voice is added in the system's settings: in Windows, in the speech settings; on a Mac, the voice is downloaded from Apple; on Android, as the voice data of the language | «Голос добавляют в настройках системы: в Windows — в настройках речи, на Mac голос загружается у Apple, в Android — как данные голоса для языка» | "This device", at a language without a voice: in words, without a link (privacy 1, 7 spec) |
| the permission on Android and Linux | On this device the system chooses the voice, and the browser can't tell whether it speaks on the device or sends the text over the network. | the notices of third-party code | Licenses and third-party notices; Notices (NOTICE); License (LICENSE) | «Лицензии и уведомления о сторонних программах»; «Уведомления (NOTICE)»; «Лицензия (LICENSE)» | the page of notices: the files' names are not translated |
| the module's license | License; Licenses of modules; The module's license and notices; Close | «Лицензия»; «Лицензии модулей»; «Лицензия и уведомления модуля»; «Закрыть» | the button of the module's card, the header and the heading of the license page (R32) |
| what is on the license page | Module; Version; License; The module has no file NOTICE; No file LICENSE came with the module | «Модуль»; «Версия»; «Лицензия»; «Файла NOTICE у модуля нет»; «Файл LICENSE с модулем не пришёл» | the module's license page; the files' headings as on the page of notices |
| the profile's modules | Modules; See the modules and their licenses; Modules: {pseudonym}; Back to the modules | «Модули»; «Посмотреть модули и их лицензии»; «Модули: {pseudonym}»; «Назад к модулям» | the profile page and the page of modules in the section for adults (R32) |
| a nonconformance on the page "Accessibility" | Criteria; Why; What there is instead; When this changes | «Критерии»; «Почему»; «Что есть вместо»; «Когда это изменится» | "Accessibility" (section 3.9) |
| where to write about accessibility | If something is hard to use; Who runs this copy and how to reach them | «Если чем-то трудно пользоваться»; «Кто ведёт эту копию и как с ним связаться» | "Accessibility": the button to `#/legal/operator`, the section of the policy with the operator's contacts (R31; R46) |
| tutor | Tutor | «Наставник» | the task screen |
| the disclosure that the tutor is an AI | The tutor is an AI, not a person; The tutor is an AI | «Наставник — это ИИ, а не человек»; «Наставник — это ИИ» | the block "Tutor"; the section "My subjects" (R33) |
| the learning materials | How to use the tutor safely; About the tutor; How to use the AI tutor safely | «Как безопасно пользоваться наставником»; «О наставнике»; «Как безопасно пользоваться ИИ-наставником» | a button of "My subjects", the header and the heading of the page `#/tutor` (R33) |
| the operator from the tutor's page | Who runs the app | «Кто ведёт приложение» | the page `#/tutor`, its part "If something is wrong", to `#/legal/operator` (R46) |
| report a problem | Report a problem; Report a problem with these words; Send | «Сообщить о проблеме»; «Сообщить о проблеме в этих словах»; «Отправить» | at each reply of the tutor, for a learner `adult` (R33) |
| the kinds of problem | What is wrong with the words?; Wrong; Unclear; Not right for the learner's age; Harmful, dangerous or upsetting; Asks about something personal, or talks of it; Something else | «Что не так со словами?»; «Неверно»; «Непонятно»; «Не по возрасту ученика»; «Вредно, опасно или расстраивает»; «Спрашивает о личном или говорит о нём»; «Другое» | the report's form, `ProblemKind` in the contract's order |
| the report's text | What else to say, if anything; Up to 1000 characters. Please write no names in it… | «Что ещё сказать — если нужно»; «До 1000 знаков. Имён здесь писать не нужно…» | the report's form: the request to write no names (R123 spec) |
| the report sent | Thank you: the report is sent. It will be reviewed by {date} | «Спасибо: сообщение отправлено. Его рассмотрят до {date}» | the response of `ReportProblem`, the day `review_by` |
| the reasons of "unreadable" | Nothing is written; Write it the way the task asks; It can't be made out; It can be read two ways; There is no final answer; There is more than one final answer | «Ничего не написано»; «Записать так, как просит задача»; «Не разобрать»; «Читается двояко»; «Нет итогового ответа»; «Итоговых ответов несколько» | after the check: `blank`, `notation`, `illegible`, `ambiguous`, `no_final_answer`, `several_final_answers` (R21) |
| "Check" disabled, the sheet empty | Write an answer on the sheet | «Записать ответ на листе» | the task screen |
| the tutor's words came | The tutor's words came | «Пришли слова наставника» | the line `role="status"` (section 3.9) |
| a workbook line | Line {n} | «Строка {n}» | after the check of a workbook |
| the task screen | Task | «Задача» | the heading of the task screen (R20) |
| the mode, a group of buttons | Mode | «Режим» | the task screen |
| an item without a text | Item {n} | «Пункт {n}» | the task screen |
| an item's answer slot | Answer {n} | «Ответ {n}» | the task screen, at an item with several answer slots |
| "Check" disabled | Choose an answer in each item | «Выбрать ответ в каждом пункте» | the task screen |
| the items not right | Not right yet: {count} item(s) of {total} | «Пока не верно: {count} пункт / пункта / пунктов из {total}» | after the check |
| the task's solution | Solution | «Решение» | after "Correct" |
| the same task again | Try it again | «Решить ещё раз» | after the end of the attempt |
| home from the task screen | Back to My subjects | «К моим предметам» | after the end of the attempt |
| open attempt | There is a task in progress; Continue the task | «Есть начатая задача»; «Продолжить задачу» | "My subjects" |
| why the attempt is closed | It was answered correctly; Its solution was shown; Another task was begun; Nothing was done on it for 7 days; It doesn't suit this profile any more; It was finished with Done | «На неё уже дан верный ответ»; «Её решение уже показано»; «Начата другая задача»; «С ней ничего не делали 7 дней»; «Она больше не подходит этому профилю»; «Она закончена кнопкой «Готово»» | "This task is already over" |
| steps | Steps | «Шаги» | the strip of steps |
| the step's place | done; now; later | «пройден»; «сейчас»; «дальше» | the strip of steps |
| chips | What we know; What to find | «Что известно»; «Что найти» | the panel of steps |
| techniques | Techniques | «Приёмы» | the panel of steps |
| the exit condition's line | Mark what we know and what to find; Write an answer in each slot | «Отметить, что известно и что найти»; «Записать ответ в каждую ячейку» | the panel of steps |

The canvas's captions — "Pen", "Eraser", "Undo", "Clear", "Draw with finger", "Up", "Down", "Next", "Demo", and since 0.3.13 "Zoom in", "Zoom out", "Left" and "Right", the names of the regions "Sheet", "Workbook" and "Worked example" and of the rows "Sheet buttons", "Workbook buttons" and "Worked example buttons" — are held by the table of `canvas` (R36 spec; R33, R42 canvas; the 2026-11-12 amendment to R11; sources 2.4, 8.1). Since v0.16.3 of `spec` the four words of zoom and of scrolling sideways stand in the table of words of architecture 6 spec too — "Zoom in", "Zoom out", "Left" and "Right", for `canvas` and `app` (the 2026-11-12 amendment to R90 spec) —, and they match the words of canvas 0.3.15 (R38). The strings of `app` that speak of them take the same words, from the table of words of architecture 6 spec: the words that more than one UI package shows are held by it, not by this glossary (R91 spec). A word of `app` that another package will need too goes there as a row «в реестр spec». "In order" is the screen's word for the mark that the documents call "sequential" (R58 spec).

**The name of a tutor's mark** is read by the screen reader, and `app` writes it in its own words (concepts 15 spec), in the screen's language: "Tutor's mark 1 of 2". On a workbook the line and the box are added to it: "Tutor's mark 1 of 2: line 3, box 2". On a handwriting sheet the place cannot be named in words: a group label is an internal letter of the device (concepts 15 spec), and a box in fractions of the crop has no words. The mark relates to the tutor's question, and the name leads to it by the number. The element will not accept an empty name (R26 canvas). From `api` come the mark's kind and its place, and the contract carries no reference to the tutor's phrase: the mark relates to the words of the reply that brought it (R97 spec).

**A path: a Russian interface over an English task.** A learner of the category `under_13`, the language in the profile `ru`. A module of olympiad maths, the explanation language `en`, the task `ladder.yaml` (concepts 16.1 spec; section 3.3).

| What is on the screen | Language | `lang` |
|---|---|---|
| the module's card in "My subjects" — the name and the line saying the module is in English | the name English, the line Russian | `en` on the name |
| the buttons «Проверить», «Подсказка», «По шагам», «Самостоятельно», «Шаг готов» | Russian | `ru` from `<html>` |
| the task statement — the canvas shows it above the sheet (R51 spec) | English | the element sets it by the manifest |
| the step "Understand the task" and the chips "The rungs are 30 cm apart." | English | `en` on the block |
| the step's line «Отметить, что известно и что найти» | Russian | `ru` |
| the canvas's captions «Перо», «Ластик», «Выше», «Ниже» | Russian | `lang="ru"` on the element |
| the verdict «Пока нет» | Russian | `ru` |
| the tutor's question | English | `en` on the block |
| the mark's name «Пометка наставника 1 из 1» | Russian | `ru` |

The screen reader reads each text with its pronunciation, and voicing reads the question in an English voice and «Пока нет» in a Russian one (section 3.5).

**Where the prototype diverges** ([the prototype's README](../spec/draft/README.md)): the prototype's section for adults has "Language of the lessons: Same as this app", while the module sets the language of the lessons, and format v0 has no translations (R46 spec). The documents take precedence.

**Tests.**
- In Node: the plural categories of each dictionary are those that `Intl.PluralRules(language).resolvedOptions().pluralCategories` gives, and `other` is always there; the lookup by truncation — `ru-RU` and `ru-Latn-RU` give `ru`, `en-GB` gives `en`, and `kk` and `be` give English; the profile's field with no value and with an unknown tag; the language of the texts from `api` is in the request; the generic string for an unknown code.
- In Chromium (section 3.1): `<html lang>` is the screen's language; the blocks of the module's text and of the tutor's words have the explanation language, through `:lang()`, as in `canvas`; the canvas element has the `lang` of the screen's language; the screens in the pseudo-language and in Russian at a width of 320 CSS px have no sideways scrolling and no clipped text (section 3.9).

**Code** — since T19, 2026-11-10 (R17):
- the dictionaries are `src/i18n/en.ts` and `src/i18n/ru.ts`, the type is `src/i18n/types.ts`: `Translation` holds the keys and the language's plural categories, and `SamePlaces` the placeholders by the string literals, and a Russian dictionary without a string, with an extra key or with another placeholder name does not pass `tsc`. The categories are `CATEGORIES`, `en` has `one` and `other`, `ru` has `one`, `few`, `many` and `other`; a test checks them against `Intl.PluralRules`;
- the words are `src/i18n/words.ts`: `text` fills the placeholders by name and takes values only for a key that carries them, and `Intl.PluralRules` chooses the plural form, and an unknown category is `other`; the placeholder element is `src/i18n/Phrase.tsx`; `Intl` with `[language, "en"]` is `src/i18n/intl.ts`; the words of a code are `keyFor` in `src/i18n/codes.ts`, and an unknown code is `common.unknown`;
- the language is `src/i18n/language.ts`: the lookup by truncation, the device's language, and the learner's language — the profile's field, otherwise the device's language. Since T37 the learner's screens — the profile's header and the screens under it — are in the learner's language: `src/i18n/LearnerLanguage.tsx` wraps them, and one place writes `<html lang>`, `src/i18n/page.ts` — the device's language from `Boot`, while the learner's screens do not hold their own (R29). The profile's language is set by the profile page of the section for adults, with the radio buttons "English" and «Русский», each with its own `lang`. `Boot` reads the choice from the device settings together with the request for `/deployment.json` and draws the first screen once it has read it, but waits no longer than a second; in a frame, only `navigator.languages`. The choice is the buttons of `src/i18n/LanguageButtons.tsx` in a `fieldset` with the legend "Language", each with the language's name in that language itself and with its own `lang`, the chosen one `aria-pressed`; the other tabs learn of the choice, if it was written, through one permanent `BroadcastChannel` `novatrail-settings` of the tab, and the chosen button is visible without color too — underlined and bold. Since T22 the buttons stand on the sign-in screen, and before that they stood on the home screen, and Google's sign-in window is in the screen's language: `Boot` sets `languageCode` of Firebase Auth at each change of language (R19);
- the built `index.html` has `<noscript>` in English and Russian, the Russian paragraph with `lang="ru"`;
- the pseudo-language is `src/test/pseudo.ts`, not in the build: English with diacritics, one and a half times longer, with the placeholders as they are.

**Later.** Each new interface language — stage 5 (product 11 spec) — is a dictionary, a check of a voice for it (section 3.5) and the same language in the tables of the UI packages: each package carries each interface language (R91 spec). Right-to-left writing comes with the first such interface or module language: then `dir` will stand beside `lang`.

### 3.9. Accessibility

Decided by R11. Accessibility is when everyone can use the interface, including people with impairments of sight, hearing or motor skills; it is not the opening of a skill and not access to content (concepts 4, 15 spec). The learners of the slice are children from 6 and adults, on a tablet and a computer (product 3, 10 spec).

**The standard** is WCAG 2.2, W3C Recommendation 12 December 2024 (sources 8.1):
- level AA in full, but for the declared nonconformances below;
- beyond it, two AAA criteria: 2.5.5 Target Size (Enhanced) — touch targets no smaller than 44 × 44 CSS px, as in `canvas` (R36 spec) — and 2.3.3 Animation from Interactions — motion that an action starts is turned off by `prefers-reduced-motion`;
- AAA in full, no: W3C itself does not advise it. WCAG 3 is a Working Draft, and until it is a Recommendation, `app` does not look at it (sources 8.1).

WCAG 2.2 AA covers WCAG 2.1 AA, which the laws require where they name a level, but for 4.1.1 Parsing — in 2.2 it is "Obsolete and removed". The beta's markets are not chosen (П-2), so the standard is chosen by the learners, and the laws are below.

**Declared nonconformances** — the page "Accessibility" names them.

| What | Criteria | Why it does not meet them | What there is instead | When it goes away |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| an answer in handwriting — an answer slot `anywhere` and `sheet`, a workbook's box | 2.1.1 Keyboard, A; 2.5.1 Pointer Gestures, A; 2.5.7 Dragging Movements, AA | handwriting is an input technique, and the function is entering an answer: "the input technique (handwriting) requires path-dependent input but the underlying function (text input) does not" (sources 8.1) | the choice of an option `choice`, by tapping and from the keyboard; a draft drawing is under the exception of 2.1.1 anyway | with a second way of answering, О-25 |
| the print of the workbook's lines — the numbers, the pieces of text and the demo's values in the boxes — does not take the user's text spacing | 1.4.12 Text Spacing, AA | `all: initial` takes the inherited spacing off it, and each piece is fitted to its own width of the layout: the place of a piece and of a box is set by the page rule of `spec`, `format/page.yaml`, not by the device's font (architecture 3.10 canvas) | the print gets larger, up to 200 %, by the canvas's buttons "Zoom in" and "Zoom out"; the statement above the workbook takes the spacing | no date: only if the page rule of `spec` gives the lines room for spacing (В77 `canvas`) |
| sending an answer by "Check" — without confirmation and without undo | 3.3.4 Error Prevention, AA | an answer is "user test responses": it gets a verdict at once and goes into evidence, and 3.3.4 requires reversal, checking or confirmation; a confirmation before each check would get in a child's way | a wrong answer does not end the attempt: after "Not yet" the learner corrects the work and checks again, and an unreadable work is returned by "unreadable" (R56 spec) | with a second way of answering, if it allows correcting an answer before the verdict: the model always counts a submission whose verdict the learner saw, and only one undone before the verdict may go uncounted (О-25, added 2026-11-07; the 2026-11-10 amendment to R11) |

Scrolling the sheet with two fingers is a multipoint gesture, and 2.5.1 and 2.5.7 are held by the buttons "Up" and "Down" of the canvas's row, and on an enlarged sheet by "Left" and "Right" (R33 canvas; О-24). With canvas 0.3.13 the print of the workbook's lines grows up to 200 %, and the nonconformance 1.4.4 is no more; and 3.1.2, which the canvas declares for an element without the attribute `lang`, never happens with `app`: it always sets the attribute (the 2026-11-12 amendment to R11). `spec` decided the same: the 2026-11-12 amendment and the second amendment of that date to R90 spec tell `app`, with canvas 0.3.14 and newer, to take only 1.4.12 onto the page "Accessibility" (R38). What is declared and what is removed was checked on the element itself — the tests `src/canvas/canvas.browser.test.tsx` —, not only by the documents of `canvas`. A limit of the canvas, not a nonconformance: in a low window the element's row of buttons without a height does not stick and goes away with scrolling (1.4.10 and 2.4.11; T92 `canvas`), and "Up" and "Down" are at hand while it is visible. `app` gives the element no height by the window: the page scrolls with one finger outside the sheet, by the scroll bar and by the keys, and the row comes back with scrolling up; checking this on devices is T51 (the 2026-11-12 amendment to R11).

The page "Accessibility" is among the legal pages of `app` (architecture 3.13 spec), and where it stands is decided by the epic E3 together with the legal texts (section 3.10). The standard, these nonconformances and what replaces them are properties of the build, the same in all copies, and their text is in the dictionaries of `app`, in the screen's language (section 3.8). Whom to contact is the copy's operator: `api` names the operator (R115 spec), not the build. The EAA expects such information "in the general terms and conditions, or equivalent document" (sources 8.1). The words follow the rules of section 3.8.

Since T40 the page exists — `#/legal/accessibility`, among the legal pages `#/legal`, visible before sign-in too (R31; section 3.10). On it are the standard; the three places where `app` does not meet it yet, by the table above, each with the criteria, why, what there is instead and when this changes; how this is checked — CI, and there have been no checks by people yet (Д10); and whom to contact. The contract has no field for the operator's contact: the operator's name and contacts are the section Who we are of their privacy policy, which the copy's `api` serves and the rule `legal.operator` holds in every version of it (R115, R131 spec; the 2026-11-12 amendment to R115 spec, by В55 of this repository, which rejected a field). Since T58 the page leads by the button "Who runs this copy and how to reach them" to the page `#/legal/operator`, which shows that section (R46; section 3.10), with a request not to write a child's personal data. The test `src/legal/LegalPages.browser.test.tsx` checks the set against the 2026-11-12 amendment to R11: 2.1.1, 2.5.1, 2.5.7, 1.4.12 and 3.3.4 are there, and 1.4.4 and 3.1.2 are not. The author proofreads the page's words.

**The theme's colors** are the author's decision at T19, 2026-11-10 (R17), in `src/theme/theme.css`. The contrast is by the WCAG formula, against the background and against the surface:

| Token | Color | Where | Contrast |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--nt-background` | `#ffffff` | the background | — |
| `--nt-surface` | `#f3f5f9` | the surface — the offer to reload, later the cards | — |
| `--nt-text` | `#1a1c22` | text | 17.03:1 and 15.60:1 |
| `--nt-text-muted` | `#4b5263` | secondary text | 7.82:1 and 7.16:1 |
| `--nt-border` | `#6b7280` | the borders of fields and cards | 4.83:1 and 4.43:1 |
| `--nt-primary` | `#1d4ed8` | buttons, what is chosen, the text of an unchosen button | 6.70:1 and 6.14:1; white `--nt-on-primary` on it, 6.70:1 |
| `--nt-focus` | `#1d4ed8` | the focus ring, 3 px with an offset of 2 px | 6.70:1 and 6.14:1 |
| `--nt-correct` | `#15803d` | "Correct", with a sign | 5.02:1 and 4.60:1 |
| `--nt-not-yet` | `#b45309` | "Not yet", with a sign: amber, not red — the verdict does not punish (product 7 spec) | 5.02:1 and 4.60:1 |
| `--nt-error` | `#b91c1c` | an error of the PIN and of the own key | 6.47:1 and 5.93:1 |
| `--nt-mark` | `#2563eb` | marks — the default color of the canvas's marks | 5.17:1 |

The canvas gets the same colors by its own properties: `--novatrail-canvas-ink` is the text, `--novatrail-canvas-paper` the background, `--novatrail-canvas-mark` the marks. The font is the system one, without a file of its own (R11). The theme is light, `color-scheme: light`.

**How `app` holds the criteria** that matter most to this application:

| Criterion | What `app` does |
|---|---|
| 1.1.1 Non-text Content, A | a module's picture has its `alt`, and an empty `alt` only a decoration ([the format text](../spec/format/README.md), section 14); an icon beside a caption is `aria-hidden` |
| 1.3.1 Info and Relationships, A; 4.1.2 Name, Role, Value, A | buttons, lists and headings are HTML elements; the strips of steps and of segments are lists with `aria-current`, and a segment that does not open is `aria-disabled` with a line saying why (sections 3.3 and 3.4); `app` gives the canvas element's name to the element itself (architecture 3.3 canvas) |
| 1.3.4 Orientation, AA | the orientation is not locked |
| 1.4.1 Use of Color, A | the verdict of an answer slot, a step and a segment passed are also shown by a word or a sign, not only by color |
| 1.4.3 Contrast (Minimum), AA; 1.4.11 Non-text Contrast, AA | text no less than 4.5:1, borders, the focus ring and marks 3:1; the theme is light |
| 1.4.4 Resize Text, AA; 1.4.10 Reflow, AA; 1.4.12 Text Spacing, AA | sizes are in `rem`; zoom is not forbidden — neither `maximum-scale` nor `user-scalable=no`; at a width of 320 CSS px, no sideways scrolling, and the canvas's sheet is a two-dimensional layout, which 1.4.10 excepts, and the canvas's buttons "Zoom in" and "Zoom out" enlarge it up to 200 % (1.4.4); the text spacing of the print of the workbook's lines is the nonconformance 1.4.12 above |
| 2.1.1 Keyboard, A; 2.1.2 No Keyboard Trap, A | everything but handwriting is from the keyboard; the canvas's row follows the APG (R36 spec) |
| 2.2.1 Timing Adjustable, A | there is one time limit — the closing of the owner profile, and the adult sets its term (section 3.6); the learner has no timers (product 7 spec; section 3.3) |
| 2.2.2 Pause, Stop, Hide, A; 2.3.1 Three Flashes or Below Threshold, A; 2.3.3 Animation from Interactions, AAA | the flashing of "Check" is one per pause, shorter than 5 s, no more often than 3 times a second, and with `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce` a still highlight (section 3.3); the transitions of screens with it are without motion |
| 2.4.2 Page Titled, A; 2.4.3 Focus Order, A | each screen has its own document title in the screen's language; on a change of screen the focus is on its heading `h1`, on a change of step on the step's name, on a change of the lesson's segment on the segment's heading `h2`; while the learner writes, the focus does not move |
| 2.4.7 Focus Visible, AA; 2.4.11 Focus Not Obscured (Minimum), AA | the focus ring is visible; the sticky header sets `--novatrail-canvas-top` (R28 canvas), and the sticky bottom bar `scroll-padding`, and they do not cover the focus |
| 2.5.3 Label in Name, A | the caption on a button of `app` is always visible, and the accessible name is its text; an icon is beside the caption, not instead of it; a chip of the strip of segments on a narrow screen shows its digit, and its name is the digit and the segment's kind, and begins with what is visible (R23) |
| 2.5.5 Target Size (Enhanced), AAA | each target of `app` is no smaller than 44 × 44 CSS px |
| 3.1.1 Language of Page, A; 3.1.2 Language of Parts, AA | section 3.8 |
| 3.2.2 On Input, A | the language and the settings are changed by a button, not by a choice in a list that would change the screen itself |
| 3.3.1 Error Identification, A; 3.3.3 Error Suggestion, AA | an error of the PIN and of the own key is in words at the field, tied to it by `aria-describedby`; the PIN names how many tries are left and when input opens — by the response `PinCheck` and the refusal `CAUSE_PIN_LOCKED` (R98 spec; section 3.6) |
| 3.3.4 Error Prevention (Legal, Financial, Data), AA | "Erase everything on this device", erasing a profile and an essay, "Remove key" and signing out are confirmed; sending an answer — "user test responses" — is the nonconformance above |
| 3.3.7 Redundant Entry, A | the own key is entered again when the owner profile has closed — this is the exception "required to ensure the security of the content" |
| 3.3.8 Accessible Authentication (Minimum), AA | sign-in is on Google's pages; the PIN field accepts pasting, the password manager is not blocked, and there are no `autocomplete` hints (section 3.6) |
| 4.1.3 Status Messages, AA | `role="status"` is on the verdict, "The tutor's words came", a "Continue" that has appeared and "Your work isn't saved on this device"; the tutor's full text does not go into the live region. The line "no voice" by the button "Read aloud" is not a status but its description, `aria-describedby`: it stands from the start rather than appearing, and a status at each button would be read on each screen (R36) |

**Beyond WCAG, for children.**
- The caption is always visible, and the icon beside it helps whoever does not read well yet (R78 spec).
- Voicing of the tutor's words and the button "Read aloud" (section 3.5): for the youngest, voicing is on by default.
- There are no timers and no competitions (product 7 spec), and the flashing at a pause is one.
- Voicing and a screen reader would speak at once, and `app` does not know whether a screen reader is running. So the tutor's words do not go into the live region whole, and the section for adults advises whoever listens to a screen reader to turn voicing off.

**UI packages.** The standard of R11 is the standard of all UI packages, `app`, `canvas` and `skill-map`: so R90 spec decided on the recommendation of `app`, and О-24 is solved. `app` could not bind another repository (RULES.md, Across repositories), and `spec` can. What a package does not meet yet, it declares itself, and `app` names it on the page "Accessibility". In every package the caption beside an icon is visible (the second 2026-11-02 amendment to R78 spec). What follows from the standard for each (R90 spec):
- `canvas` — zoom of the sheet up to 200% with scrolling in two directions, which 1.4.10 allows the sheet, — is there since 0.3.13, and the nonconformance to 1.4.4 is gone; the lines and boxes of the workbook in the accessibility tree; the keyboard into the sheet and out of it; the row of buttons does not stick when the window is low; the frames of `exportWork()` do not change with the theme and forced colors; its own axe-core;
- `skill-map` — a text alternative to the graph, `lang` on the name of each skill, mastery not by color alone, the keyboard and moving without dragging;
- WebKit bug 277290: in Safari a scroll container without focusable content cannot be scrolled from the keyboard (О-24). `app` scrolls with the page, and gives a scroll container of its own only with `tabindex="0"`, the role `region` and a name.

**What CI checks** — the recipes `ci-lint` and `ci-test` (section 3.1):
- Biome — the rules of the group `a11y`, by default: of 38, 37 are on (sources 8.1);
- the screens' tests find elements by role and name, `getByRole`, so a button without a name fails the test, and `toHaveAccessibleName` checks the name;
- checks of its own in Chromium, WebKit and Firefox (R45): each target of `app` is no smaller than 44 × 44 CSS px, at a width of 320 px too; `lang` on the blocks of the module's text and of the tutor's words — through `:lang()`; the screens in the pseudo-language and in Russian at a width of 320 px — without sideways scrolling and without clipped text; Vitest projects with `forcedColors: "active"`, as at `canvas`, in Chromium and Firefox, and with `reducedMotion: "reduce"`, where there is no blinking;
- the contrast of the theme's colors — in Node, by the WCAG formula: text — no less than 4.5:1, borders, the focus ring and marks — 3:1;
- axe-core 4.14.0 on every screen, except the subtree of the canvas element: `canvas` checks it in its own CI, and in `app` a violation of the package would fail others' pull requests. axe-core is under MPL-2.0, as a named exception `dev` (section 3.1). In Deque's words, it finds "on average 57% of WCAG issues" (sources 8.1).

**What a person checks**: "Tools cannot check all accessibility aspects automatically" (sources 8.1). Before the first child and with each new screen — Д10 in table 4 of RUN.md:

| Device | With what | What |
|---|---|---|
| iPad, Safari and the app from the "Home Screen" | VoiceOver, Switch Control, Voice Control | the task screen and the lesson, the choice of the learner, the PIN; voicing together with VoiceOver |
| iPad, Safari | page zoom, "Smart Invert" | nothing is clipped, marks are visible |
| Android, Chrome | TalkBack | the same as with VoiceOver |
| Windows, Chrome and Firefox | NVDA, Narrator; forced colors | names, focus order, the language of parts; borders and buttons in forced colors |
| Mac, Safari | VoiceOver; keyboard only | the whole path without a mouse |
| a computer, any browser | zoom 200%, width 320 CSS px, text spacing 1.4.12 | nothing is lost |

**Code** — since T19, 2026-11-10 (R17):
- the theme is `src/theme/theme.css`, and `src/boot/Boot.tsx` includes it: sizes in `rem`, a target no smaller than 2.75rem, the focus ring an `outline` by `:focus-visible`, long words wrapped by `overflow-wrap: anywhere`, the button's color transition only under `prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference`, and `forced-color-adjust: none` nowhere. The contrast is `src/theme/contrast.ts`, and a test in Node reads the tokens from `theme.css` itself and checks each pair of the table above;
- the screen is `src/app/Screen.tsx`: `<main lang>`, `<h1 tabIndex={-1}>`, the document title "{screen} — NovaTrail" in the screen's language — on the home screen just "NovaTrail" — and focus on `h1` when one screen has replaced another, — not on the first screen of the page, under StrictMode too, and not while the screen stays the same;
- the checks are `src/test/a11y.ts`: `axe-core` by the rules with the tags `wcag2a`, `wcag2aa`, `wcag21a`, `wcag21aa` and `wcag22aa` — the standard of R11, without best-practice, — outside the subtree of `novatrail-canvas`; targets no smaller than 44 × 44 CSS px; the page without sideways scrolling, and at each element content no wider than the element itself. `src/a11y/screens.browser.test.tsx` runs them on each screen — "in a frame", "no connection", "not set up", "erased", "can't be read", the home screen with the language buttons and the offer to reload — in `en`, `ru` and the pseudo-language, at a width of 1024 and 320 CSS px, and checks the name of each button against its caption. The projects `forced colors` and `reduced motion` are files of their own in `src/a11y/`;
- `lang` on the blocks of the module's text — since T23 on the module's name, since T24 on the step, the chips, the techniques, the items, the options and the solution, — and the task screens "by steps", "On my own", after "Not yet" and solved are in `screens.browser.test.tsx`; since T27 there too the lesson at an explanation, a worked example, a task and a task not begun, the lesson end screen and "A lesson in progress" (R23). The blinking of "Check" since T24: the project `browser` keeps the animation — three flashes of 1 s each, — and the project `reduced motion` a still outline without animation (R20).
- what the canvas declares for the page "Accessibility" — since T36 on the element itself, in `src/canvas/canvas.browser.test.tsx`: after "Zoom in" all the way, the sheet and the print of the workbook are twice as wide as when fitted, with the area "Workbook", "Left" and "Right" (1.4.4 met), and the test of the built page — the same under the CSP header; under the text spacing of 1.4.12 on the page and on the element, the statement takes it, and the widths of the pieces of print stay the same (1.4.12 — a nonconformance); the area «Лист» and "Sheet" are in the attribute's language, without a `lang` of their own (`app` has no 3.1.2). A canvas that changes any of this will fail the test, and the page's list is read anew (the 2026-11-12 amendment to R11).

There are no forced colors on iPad and Mac: WebKit always answers `forced-colors: none` (sources 8.1; measurements 2). So they are checked on Windows.

**Laws** — what was read (sources 8.1). The beta's markets are not chosen, and what of this concerns NovaTrail is the question П-25 in the register of `spec`. The platform has no lawyer: legal questions are decided by the author on Claude's analysis (R112 spec).

| Where | Law | What it requires | What the primary source did not show |
|---|---|---|---|
| EU | European Accessibility Act, Directive 2019/882, — from 28 June 2025 | accessibility of e-commerce services — "the online sale of any product or service" — and information about it; microenterprises are exempt. Learning is not among the services | the countries' laws transposing it; whether the standard EN 301 549 V4.1.1, which equates itself with WCAG 2.2 AA, is named in the Official Journal — as of 7 September 2026, V3.2.1, WCAG 2.1 AA, was in force |
| USA | ADA, Title II, the 2024 rule | WCAG 2.1 AA for the websites and apps of state and local government bodies, including through contracts, — from 26 April 2027 and 2028 | — |
| USA | ADA, Title III; Section 508 | for Title III the DOJ has no technical standard; Section 508 — WCAG 2.0 AA for federal agencies | — |
| UK | Equality Act 2010 | reasonable adjustments by the service provider, in advance; for the public sector gov.uk requires WCAG 2.2 AA | — |
| Russia | the federal law «181-ФЗ»; the standard «ГОСТ Р 52872-2019» | on private websites «181-ФЗ» is silent; the GOST follows WCAG 2.1 and is voluntary; resolution No. 102 of 7 February 2026 is about the websites of state bodies | whether the GOST is in force now: docs.cntd.ru did not answer |

A public school in the USA that takes `app` in self-hosting or by contract answers for WCAG 2.1 AA itself, and the declared nonconformances will not cover that for it (П-25).

**Later.** The second way to answer is О-25; the dark theme — by `prefers-color-scheme`, once the same test checks its contrast; a font for children is a question of its own: fonts with Cyrillic are almost all under OFL-1.1, and it is not in the list of licenses (R12 spec).

### 3.10. What else and in what order

The result of the detailing, T08. The card's items "To decide when detailed" are decided — sections 3.1–3.9. What R05, R06, R08, R09 and R11 left to the result — the screen after the check, the conversation line, where an essay is reread, the PIN of the section for adults, the choice of the learner, the legal texts and the place of the page "Accessibility" — the result does not decide but names below, with the epic that builds them: this is the layout of screens, not how the app is built. What the conversation line is, the card decided: in the first slice, buttons of requests, without the learner's own text (architecture 3.1 spec). Here is the rest that the card assigns to `app` (architecture 3.1 spec), the open questions of `spec` about the screens of `app` (section 2), and what each epic of code takes from the decisions. The epics are in tables 1 and 2 of RUN.md:
- **E2 — the first app** — done 2026-11-12, archive — [plan/E2.md](plan/E2.md): the first build of `app` with `api` at the author's, locally, without children and without deployment (R76 spec). The path of the first app — sign-in, "My subjects", a lesson, a task, the check of handwriting, a hint and the choice of the next one — was walked with `api` on 2026-11-12, without the tutor's words (T30; T42 `api`), and the first signed release of the build is v0.1.0, 2026-11-12, and `release-verify` accepts it (T31; the 2026-11-12 amendment to R25). What E2 left: completing the path of T30 by hand — Д14 in table 4 of RUN.md; the tutor's words — row 5.7 of [COORDINATION.md](../spec/COORDINATION.md), and until them the rung's fallback text; WebKit and Firefox in CI — В50, work in IndexedDB as bytes — В51; the notices of third-party code on the legal pages — E3 (R25); revoking the Google token on deletion — the row В52 `api`, E4; the checks on devices — Д4–Д10 and Д13;
- **E3 — the first slice with adult learners:** deployment, own key, voicing, the skill map, the section for adults. The author chose it on 2026-11-12 (T31) and planned it the same day: the tasks T35–T52 are in table 1 of [RUN.md](../RUN.md). They wait for the neighbors:
  - own key — for the methods of `api`;
  - essays and the workbook — for their admission in `api`;
  - the quota — for the epic E6 of `api`: it was done 2026-11-12, and T49 took its outcomes and refusals onto the screens (R37); the quota share — `GetQuota` — the contract gave from v0.16.3 of `spec`, and T53 shows it: to the learner in "My subjects", to the adult in the section for adults (R40), and `api` will fill it with its move to the tag (row 6.13 of [COORDINATION.md](../spec/COORDINATION.md));
  - deployment — for the first rollout of `infra`;
  - the modules for an anchor on the real `api` — for `api` to fill `teaching_modules` by Д210 of RUN.md `spec`: the field is in the contract since v0.15.1;
- **E4 — before the first child:** children's profiles, consent, export and deletion (repositories 6 spec; R116, R119, R121 spec).
