Of a child consent a minimal record remains for 5 years (R117 spec; П-14 closed), and of an essay the server keeps only the submission ends and the marks of the criteria (R87 spec; П-19 closed). Deleting the Firebase user does not remove the "Sign in with Google" connection in the owner's Google account — checked 2026-11-12 on the dev project (T42 `api`); `app` could remove it by revoking, on deletion, the Google token that it gets at sign-in by the window — the row В52 `api` «в реестр spec», and until `spec` decides it, the connection remains | E4 |
| legal texts | pages of `app` (architecture 3.13 spec), by `LegalService.GetLegalTexts` (R130 spec); in a self-hosting copy, the texts of its operator (R115 spec); only in English, and the line that the text is in English and the gist of a consent are in the screen's language (privacy 7 spec; R116 spec; section 3.8); the page "Accessibility" (R11) ; the pages of the policy and the terms are the texts `privacy-policy` and `terms-of-use`, Markdown without HTML, pictures and links (Д160 and Д166 in table 4 of RUN.md `spec`) ; since v0.15.0 of `spec` a version may have a translation without force of its own, now `privacy-policy` version 2 into Russian (R141 spec; the 2026-11-11 amendment to R130 spec) | a link with the word "privacy" on the start screen and in the settings, and a new version of the policy before it applies, with a way to withdraw consent (Д204 in table 4 of RUN.md `spec`); the texts in force since v0.15.0 of `spec` are `privacy-policy` version 2, `terms-of-use` and `storage-consent` version 1, and the family texts (R138, R141 spec; П-27 closed by R137 spec) | E2 — the account's texts on the invite code screen, T32 (R27); since T40 — the pages `#/legal`, visible before sign-in too: the policy, the terms and the consent to keeping works — the texts in force, open, — "Accessibility" and the notices of third-party code from the bundle; the button with the word "privacy" is on the sign-in screen and on the first page of the section (R31; below, "Legal pages"); since T58 — the next version before it applies, by `next_texts` of the contract v0.17.1, now `privacy-policy` version 3 from 2026-11-27: on its page `#/legal/<id>/next`, in place on the invite code's screen and in the consent to keeping works, and on the choice of the learner and the section's first page; and who runs the copy — `#/legal/operator`, the section Who we are of the policy in force (R46) |
| the licenses of modules | `LICENSE` and `NOTICE` of the device part are served by `api`, and R62 spec quotes from Apache-2.0, §4(a): "You must give any other recipients of the Work or Derivative Works a copy of this License". Which licenses third-party modules have is О-10 | where they are seen — at the module in "My subjects" and in the section for adults, as a page of its own, without an external link; in what language the texts of a third-party module are — В56 «в реестр spec» | E3: with deployment the package goes to others; since T41 — the module's license page `#/license/<digest>` from the card in "My subjects" and `#/adults/license/<digest>` from the profile's modules page in the section (R32; below, "Licenses of modules") |
| module widgets | the widget host of `canvas`, in an iframe sandbox (R16 spec) | — | stage 4 |

**Section for adults** — since T37, 2026-11-12 (R29); decided by the executor on the author's one-time instruction, and the author reviews it.
- **Routes:** `#/adults` is the first page, `#/adults/<page>` the others; the profile is not in the address but in the section's memory (R14). The profile page without a chosen profile — after a reload — and an unknown page lead to the first page, in their place in the history.
- **Where:** `AccountGate` shows the section in place of the choice of the learner or of the learner's screens, while the account is open. The entry is "For grown-ups" on the choice of the learner and in the profile's header; in E3 without a PIN, and the PIN will stand before it from E4.
- **The section's header:** "For grown-ups", "Back to the section" — except on the first page, — and "Close the section", home: to the choice of the learner or to "My subjects".
- **First page:** "Profiles" — a button "Profile: {pseudonym}" for each, the owner first; since T38 — "This device" and the button "See what is on this device"; "Language on this device" — the buttons of the device's language (section 3.8); since T40 — "Privacy and legal information" and the button "Read the privacy policy and other texts" to the legal pages (R31); since T58, last — "New versions of the texts" while a text has a next version, as on the choice of the learner (R46; below, "Legal pages").
- **"This device"** `#/adults/device` — since T38 (the 2026-11-12 amendment to R04): for each profile, the unfinished tasks, the space and the warning about losing works, and for the whole site, the browser's estimate, persistent storage, Safari's seven days and the "Home Screen"; "Erase the profile's data on this device" and "Erase everything on this device" — with a question. "Erase everything" is a sign-out, and on top of it the device's settings and the heartbeat database. In detail — section 3.2.
- **Profile page** `#/adults/profile` — one form with "Save": pseudonym, the default mode (section 3.3), the language of the learner's screens (section 3.8) and voicing with its speed (section 3.5). `UpdateProfile` — only with the fields that changed against what was shown; nothing changed — no request. From the answer the tab takes the settings and the keeping of works, not the attempt and the runs, and sends the profile to other tabs by the `BroadcastChannel` `novatrail-profiles`. Since T39 under the form — "Keeping works on the server" (R30): whether it is on, a line that the server does not keep works yet, and turning it on is a consent in place of the button: the text `storage-consent` through `LegalText`, the words of R127 spec and a tick of explicit consent, which holds to the version shown; `SetWorkStorage` with `ACTION_ADULT` and this version, on `CAUSE_TEXT_NOT_CURRENT` — the text anew and the tick again. Turning it off is at once, without a question. The answer goes the same way as the answer of `UpdateProfile`. Since T41 under the keeping — "Modules", a line about them and the button "See the modules and their licenses" (R32), and since T44 under them — "Progress" and the button "See the progress" (R35).
- **How it grows:** a page of the account or of the device is a part of the hash and a button on the first page, a page of the profile is a part of the hash and a button on the profile page. Proposed by R29, and the tasks themselves decide: "This device" of T38 — `#/adults/device`, and so it went in; the switch of keeping works of T39 — on the profile page, and so it went in, as a section of its own under the form (R30); the legal pages of T40 — routes of their own `#/legal`, visible before sign-in too, and the first page leads to them, and so it went in (R31); the licenses of modules of T41 — the profile's modules page `#/adults/modules` from the profile page, and the license of a version is `#/adults/license/<digest>`, a page of the section, and `#/license/<digest>` outside the section, from the card in "My subjects" (R32); progress of T44 — `#/adults/progress`, from the profile page, and so it went in, and the module's map in it is `#/adults/progress/<module ID>` (R35); own key of T46 — `#/adults/own-key`, from the first page; the quota share did not go in with T49: there was no request for the share in the contract v0.15.1 (R37); since T53 — the section "Tutor time" at the bottom of the profile page, not the first page: `GetQuota` asks for a profile (R40).
- **Code:** `src/adults/AdultSection.tsx` — the header, the first page and the choice of the page by the hash, `src/adults/ProfilePage.tsx` — the form, `src/adults/DevicePage.tsx` — "This device", since T38, `src/adults/WorkStorage.tsx` — keeping works on the server and the consent, since T39, `src/adults/ModulesPage.tsx` — the profile's modules, since T41, `src/adults/ProgressPage.tsx` and `src/adults/progress.ts` — progress, since T44, and `src/adults/order.ts` — the order of profiles; `src/account/settings.ts` — the form by the profile, what changed, the profile from the answer with the tab's attempt and runs (`withSettings`, `asGiven`) and the message to other tabs; `Session.profileChanged` and `Session.profileFailed`; `AccountGate` — the section and the channel. Checked in Chromium: the path from the choice of the learner and from the header, the request only with the changed fields, the message to another tab and its profile on the learner's screens, the default mode in an open attempt, the first page instead of a page without a profile and an unknown one; the accessibility checks — on the first page and the profile page, with an error and with a language that is not in the build, in `en`, `ru` and the pseudo-language, at 1024 and 320 px, and in forced colors. Since T38 — "This device": what is seen per profile, a profile without a database and a database that cannot be read, persistent storage, both erasures with a question and focus, and "Erase everything" through `AccountGate` to the sign-in screen; the accessibility checks — on the page and with the question before "Erase everything". Since T39 — keeping works: off by default, the consent with the text and the words of R127 spec, the version of the text shown in `SetWorkStorage`, `CAUSE_TEXT_NOT_CURRENT` — the text anew and the tick again, without a text in force — a request without `text`, turning off without a question, refusals, a failure to read the text and the focus after each step; through `AccountGate` — turning on reaching another tab, and the warning of "This device" about losing works stands with keeping turned on too; the accessibility checks — on the profile page with the consent, unticked, and with keeping turned on. Since T53 — "Tutor time", `src/adults/QuotaSection.tsx` and `src/account/quota.ts` (R40): in Node — the request by action, `unimplemented` without a reload, another failure, the learner's and the adult's lines in `en` and `ru` and the day by the device's calendar; in Chromium — the section under "Progress" with both shares and the day, a cap reached, a tariff without caps, no section from an `api` without `GetQuota`, a failure and a retry; and in "My subjects" — the line of the share above "Continue", without the account's share, the words of the refusal at a cap reached, no line without a percentage, without `GetQuota` and at a failure, and again — by "Try again" of the lists; the accessibility checks — on the profile page with a cap reached and on "My subjects" with the share.

**Legal pages** — since T40, 2026-11-12 (R31); decided by the executor on the author's one-time instruction, and the author reviews it.
- **Routes:** `#/legal` — the list, `#/legal/privacy-policy`, `#/legal/terms-of-use` and `#/legal/storage-consent` — the texts by ID, `#/legal/accessibility` and `#/legal/notices`; since T58 — `#/legal/<id>/next`, a text's next version, and `#/legal/operator`, who runs the copy (R46); an unknown page — and a family text — leads to the list. The family texts get no pages: the consent screens show them, E4.
- **Where:** `AccountGate` shows them at any step of the session, before sign-in too, in the device's language. The button "Privacy and legal information" is on the sign-in screen, under the sign-in button, and "Read the privacy policy and other texts" on the first page of the section. The header is "Back to the list" and "Close", to where they were opened from: from the section — to its first page, while its account is open, otherwise home; where from — in memory, and after a reload — home (R31). Since T58 a page opens straight from the notice of next versions on the choice of the learner and in the section, and the operator's page from the page on the AI tutor, and "Close" leads back there (R46).
- **Text page:** "Version {version}, in force since {date}", the gist, the line that the English text is in force, the translation and the body — open, `LegalContent`; the text is read at each opening, past the cache (R27); no version in force — it says so, a failure — its words and "Try again". Since T58, under the version — "Version {version} of this text applies from {date}" and "Read version {version}" while the text has a next version (R46).
- **Next version's page** `#/legal/<id>/next` — since T58 (R46): "{text}: the next version", "Version {version}, applies from {date}", "Until then, version {version} is in force", the line that a consent given in the app can be withdrawn before it applies, "Read the version in force" and the version as `LegalContent` shows the current one; without a next version — "This text has no next version now", and without a current one there is neither "until then" nor the way back.
- **Who runs this copy** `#/legal/operator` — since T58 (R46): the section Who we are of the policy in force, from its heading of the second level to the next of the first or the second, in English — `sectionOf` —, with the version and the day of the policy, the line that the English text is in force on a screen not in English, and "Read the whole privacy policy"; a policy without the section and a copy without a policy say so. The list leads to it by an item after the three texts, "Accessibility" by its button (section 3.9), the page on the AI tutor by "Who runs the app".
- **Notices of third-party code:** `NOTICE` and `LICENSE` of the build as they are, `<pre lang="en">` with line wrapping; the copy is from the bundle, not from `/NOTICE` of the hosting: `app` loads none of its parts at run time (R16 spec), and `vite preview` and a self-hosting copy might not serve `/NOTICE` (section 3.1).
- **A new version before it applies** — the promise of the section "Changes to this policy" of version 2 and Д204 `spec`, and CGS §42-520(b)(3) (sources 3.11 spec) — since T58, by `next_texts` of `GetLegalTexts` (the 2026-11-13 amendment to R130 spec), which `api` gives since its T74 (R46). In place, `NextLegalText`: on the invite code's screen each text of the account is followed by its next version, and in the consent to keeping works `storage-consent` by its own, a disclosure titled with the day, and with no current version, with no tick; the tick, `CreateAccount` and `SetWorkStorage` name the current version alone. After signing in, `NextVersions`: on the choice of the learner, under the profiles, and on the section's first page, "New versions of the texts", each with its day and "Read {text}, version {version}", and while a profile keeps its works on the server, the line that this can be switched off before then; no next version, an `api` without the field and a failure show nothing. An owner without children has only one thing to withdraw in E3 — keeping works on the server (R30): their learning rests on the contract (R116 spec), and the account's deletion is E4.
- **Code:** `src/legal/LegalPages.tsx` — the list, the pages and the header; `src/legal/notices.ts`; `LegalContent` in `src/legal/LegalText.tsx`; `PAGE_TEXTS` in `src/legal/texts.ts`; the route `LEGAL` in `src/app/hash.ts`; since T58 — `NextTextPage` and `OperatorPage` in `src/legal/LegalPages.tsx`, `NextLegalText` in `src/legal/LegalText.tsx`, `readLegal`, `nextOf` and `pageNext` in `src/legal/texts.ts`, `sectionOf` in `src/legal/body.ts`, and `src/legal/NextVersions.tsx` (R46). Checked in Chromium: the list and the header, a text on a Russian screen with the version, the gist, the translation and the body, without links, a text without a version in force and an `api` without `LegalService`, a failure and "Try again", "Accessibility" with the set of the amendment to R11, the notices byte for byte, an unknown page; through `AccountGate` — the path from the sign-in screen without signing in and from the section back to the section; the accessibility checks — on the list, the policy page with a translation and a table, "Accessibility" and the notices, in `en`, `ru` and the pseudo-language, at 1024 and 320 px; the test of the built page — under the CSP header, without violations, with one request beyond `/deployment.json` — `GetLegalTexts`. Since T58 (R46): in Node — `readLegal` with the next versions, without the field and with `unimplemented`, and `sectionOf` — up to the next heading of its level, to the end, not of the third level or another text; in the three engines — the line and the button on the text's page, the next version's page with the day, "until then", the withdrawal and the way back, without a next version and without a current one, the operator's page on a Russian screen, without the section and without a policy, and the button of "Accessibility"; the next version in place on the invite code's screen, with the tick and `CreateAccount` on the current, and in the consent to keeping works; the notice on the choice of the learner and on the section's first page, with the line on keeping works and without it, two texts with next versions of one number, none at a failure, and "Close" back; from the page on the AI tutor to the operator and back, signed in and not; the accessibility checks — on the next version's page, the operator's page, the choice of the learner with the notice and the section's first page; the test of the built page — "Accessibility", the operator's page and the policy's page, under the CSP header, with `GetLegalTexts` at each opening; the path of `walk/` — on the choice of the learner, the notice exactly when `api` gives `next_texts`.

**Licenses of modules** — since T41, 2026-11-12 (R32); decided by the executor on the author's one-time instruction, and the author reviews it.
- **What:** `LICENSE` and `NOTICE` of the device part of one version — as they are, each in `<pre lang="en">` with line wrapping, like the notices of third-party code, — and above them the module by the manifest of this version: the name in the explanation language, the version and the license by its SPDX identifier. The files are a `GET` without a token at the address of `api` ([the text of the contracts](../spec/contracts/README.md), section 15, "Files of a device part"): `200` — the text, `404` for `NOTICE` — "The module has no file NOTICE", and for `LICENSE`, without which the format does not assemble a package, — "No file LICENSE came with the module"; `410` — "This version of the module is no longer given" and `CheckPackages`; `503` — a retry by `Retry-After`; a network failure — its words and "Try again". The files are read one after the other, and the manifest separately: a manifest that is slow or does not come does not hold up the texts. There are no links on the page: a link to a file would lead to another origin (privacy 7 spec).
- **Routes:** `#/license/<digest>` and `#/adults/license/<digest>`; the package digest is an ID of content, not of a learner (R14). A route without a digest leads home or to the first page of the section, in its place in the history.
- **Where:** at the card in "My subjects" with a current version — connected and in the catalog — the button "License", described by the module's name; the page `#/license/…` is shown by `AccountGate` at any step, like the legal pages, under the header "Licenses of modules" with "Close" — home, and in the language of the learner's screens while a learner is chosen. In the section — the profile's modules page `#/adults/modules` from the profile page: the connected modules by `ListConnectedModules`, cards as in "My subjects", at each version — "License", and at a module without a current version — "Not available now" without a button; the license is a page of the section, under its header and with "Back to the modules", while a profile is chosen.
- **The language of the texts** of a third-party module the format does not name, and `app` gives them `lang="en"`: the official modules are Apache-2.0 in English (R20 spec), and the licenses of third-party ones are О-10. The language of `LICENSE` and `NOTICE` is the language of the license, and there is no field for it until the answer to О-10: so the format text will say, by the task T147 `spec` (R38).
- **Code:** `src/packages/LicensePage.tsx` — the page, its route and header; `src/packages/license.ts` — reading the files; `text` and `isDigest` in `src/packages/files.ts`; `src/adults/ModulesPage.tsx`; `src/subjects/CardHeading.tsx` — what a module card says, shared by "My subjects" and the section; the route `MODULE_LICENSE` in `src/app/hash.ts`. Checked: in Node — `200`, `404`, `410` with one `CheckPackages`, `503` and a broken-off body for `LICENSE` and `NOTICE`; in Chromium — the page with the texts as they are and `lang="en"`, a module without `NOTICE` on a Russian screen, the texts without a manifest that does not come, a withdrawn version, a `LICENSE` that is missing, a failure and "Try again", a route without a digest, the profile's modules and the path to the license and back, the license after a reload without a chosen profile; through `AccountGate` — from the card in "My subjects" in the profile's language and back, from the section in the device's language and by the address before sign-in; the accessibility checks — the cards with "License", the license page and the profile's modules, in `en`, `ru` and the pseudo-language, at 1024 and 320 px; the test of the built page — by the address before sign-in, under the CSP header, without violations and links, with requests only to `api`.

**Tutor — AI** — since T42, 2026-11-12 (R33); decided by the executor on the author's one-time instruction, and the author reviews it and proofreads the texts.
- **Disclosure:** the line "The tutor is an AI, not a person" under the heading of each block "Tutor" — at the words of a rung, "Explain differently", the correct answer words and while the tutor is writing; in "My subjects" under the lists — the section "The tutor is an AI" and the button "How to use the tutor safely". Always, not once: there is no mark "already seen" on the device.
- **Learning materials** — the page `#/tutor`, "How to use the AI tutor safely", six sections: it is AI, and the verdict is the program's; it can be wrong; what goes to AI and what does not, and not to write names and personal things; it is for learning; it is not someone to talk to about personal things; if something is wrong — "Report a problem" and the emergency service. The content is from the platform's documents, not from the providers' terms: those require materials but do not name their content (R33, the quotes with the date read 2026-11-12). `AccountGate` shows the page at any step, before sign-in too, in the language of the learner's screens while one is chosen, with the header "About the tutor" and "Close" home; there are no links on it.
- **"Report a problem"** — a button under the words in the block "Tutor", and in its place a form: "What is wrong with the words?" — six kinds in the order of `ProblemKind`, required, and "Something else" with a text; "What else to say, if anything" — up to 1000 characters, `maxLength`, with a request not to write names; lines that the words will go with the report and that in danger right now — the emergency service; "Send" and "Cancel". `ReportProblem` carries the words as the block showed them, the reply that brought them — `TUTOR_REPLY_RUNG`, `TUTOR_REPLY_EXPLAINED_DIFFERENTLY` or `TUTOR_REPLY_CORRECT_ANSWER`, — and the rung for the first two. The idempotency key is one per report, as the form holds it: a retry with the same one, an edit or a refusal with a new one. The answer is "Thank you: the report is sent. It will be reviewed by {date}" by `review_by`, and the button at these words is no longer there.
- **Who sees the button** — a learner of the category `adult`; at a profile of another category — E4, by П-26 (section 2).
- **Code:** `src/tutor/report.ts`, `src/tutor/ReportProblem.tsx`, `src/tutor/TutorPage.tsx`; the line and the place of the button — `src/task/TutorWords.tsx`, the words with their reply — `Said` in `src/task/Task.tsx`; the section in `src/subjects/MySubjects.tsx`; the route `TUTOR` in `src/app/hash.ts`. Checked: in Node — six kinds, the conditions of sending and the request with a rung and without; in Chromium — the line of disclosure and no button for a learner who is not `adult`; a report about the words of a rung — the kind, the text, the key, `review_by` and focus; a retry after a lost answer and after a failure — with the same key, and `api` holds one report; an edit — with a new key; the key at `CAUSE_STILL_GOING` and a new one after a refusal; the correct answer words without a rung; the words with which the attempt went on, and "Explain differently" with its rung; "Something else" without a text, "Cancel" with focus and an answer without a day; the section of "My subjects" and the path to the page; through `AccountGate` — the page in the profile's language and back, before sign-in by the address and an unknown part of the hash; the accessibility checks — the page, the button, the form with an error and the form after a correct answer in `en`, `ru` and the pseudo-language, at 1024 and 320 px, and the form in forced colors; the test of the built page — `#/tutor` under the CSP header, without violations and links, without requests to `api`.

**Skill map** — since T43, 2026-11-12 (R34); decided by the executor on the author's one-time instruction, and the author reviews it.
- **Routes:** `#/skill-map` — the list of maps: the connected modules `ListConnectedModules`, cards as in "My subjects", at a module with a current version — "Open the map", and without one — "Not available now" without a button; `#/skill-map/<module ID>` — the module's map. A module ID is an ID of content, not of a learner (R14); a hash with extra parts leads to the list, in its place in the history. The entry is the button "Skill map" in "My subjects" under "What's next", and from the list and the map — "All skill maps" and "Back to My subjects".
- **Map:** `GetSkillMap` of `ProgressService` with `ACTION_LEARNING` — the state of the skills and the anchors and the package digest on which it was computed, — and by this digest, from the shared cache of open views, the skill graph with tiers, `GetSkillGraph`, and the manifest, `GetManifest` (R99 spec). The element is `view="learner"`, `lang` is the language of the learner's screens, the headings of its bands are `h2` under the screen's heading "Skill map: {module}". For a module without a graph — "This subject has no skill map". The theme is `--novatrail-skill-map-paper`, `-ink` and `-accent` in `src/theme/theme.css` — the background, the text and the main color of the theme of `app`, whose pairs already hold the contrast (section 3.9).
- **Choosing a skill:** "Practise" on a skill's tile — the event `skillchoose` — sends `ChooseNext` with the skill's full ID, `<module>/skill/<skill>`, and from there as "What's next": a lesson opens, a task begins (R24). While an attempt is open, first comes the question of "Something else": "The task in progress closes…", "Go on" and "Cancel" with focus back on "Practise". While the choice is going, the status line above the map says "Opening what's next…", and another press of "Practise" does nothing; "Nothing to choose" — by the same line. "Go on", like "Cancel", returns focus to "Practise". The skill is held by `api` (R107 spec), and "What's next" and "Something else" in "My subjects" send `ChooseNext` without a skill and return the choice to the engine. The idempotency key of the choice is one per skill, until the choice has ended with an answer or a refusal. The code is the shared hook `useEngine` in `src/next/`, which `NextStep` uses too.
- **Modules for an anchor** — beside the map, as the section "Modules to connect" above it: the element does not read `teaching_modules` (Д211 in table 4 of RUN.md `spec`). These are the modules of all anchors whose mastery is "unknown" or a value the build does not know, — each once, as cards as in "My subjects", with the name from the manifest of the current version by the digest from `ListCatalog` and "Connect" — `ConnectModule`. A module that the catalog does not give the profile is "Not available now" without a button. After "Connect", in place of the button — "Connected: it is in My subjects now" with focus, and the map's state is read anew, without blinking: the anchor may become "not mastered". The list is the one there was when the map was opened, and it does not change until the next opening. The anchors are not named one by one: `app` has no names of anchors — they are in the shared taxonomy of the format's release and in the element itself.
- **For T44:** `readModuleMap` in `src/skills/map.ts` reads the map with any action, and `ModuleSkillMap` in `src/skills/SkillMap.tsx` draws the element with `view="adult"` and the level of the screen's headings — without `onChoose`: the adult does not choose a skill. And so T44 took it (R35).
- **Code:** `src/skills/` — `jsx.ts`, `map.ts`, `SkillMap.tsx` and `SkillMapScreen.tsx` — the screens and their route; `src/next/useEngine.ts`; the label — `isSequential` and `sequentialOf` in `src/subjects/modules.ts`, `src/subjects/useSequential.ts` and `CardHeading`; the route `SKILL_MAP` in `src/app/hash.ts`; the keys `skillmap.*` and `subjects.sequential` in the dictionaries. Checked: in Node — reading the map with the cache of views, without a graph and a refusal, the modules for an anchor — "unknown", "mastered", "not mastered", an unknown value and a retry, the label and its graph; in Chromium — the element's properties, attributes and event, the list of maps and the path to a map and back, `ChooseNext` with a skill and a task, the line "Opening…" and a press during the choice, "nothing to choose", the question at an open attempt with "Cancel" and "Go on" and the focus after them, a module for an anchor in `ru` — the name and "Connect", a module outside the catalog, connecting with focus and the new state, a failure of connecting and a retry, an anchor "mastered" without the section, a module without a graph, a refusal and "Try again", the label in "My subjects" and the entry to the map; the accessibility checks — the list of maps and a map with an open skill and a module for an anchor, in `en`, `ru` and the pseudo-language, at 1024 and 320 px, with the element's subtree; the test of the built page — the element under the CSP header: its styles, the lines of the graph and "Practise", without violations and without requests of its own.

**Progress in the section for adults** — since T44, 2026-11-12 (R35); decided by the executor on the author's one-time instruction, and the author reviews it.
- **Routes:** `#/adults/progress` — the progress of the profile chosen in the section, and `#/adults/progress/<module ID>` — the module's map in the adult's view; without a chosen profile — after a reload — both lead to the first page of the section, in their place in the history. The entry is the section "Progress" on the profile page, under "Modules", and the button "See the progress". The language is the device's.
- **Page:** "Progress: {pseudonym}" and a line that why each task was given is seen only here. Under it:
  - "Skill maps" — the connected modules as cards of "My subjects" with "Open the map"; the map is the element `skill-map` with `view="adult"`, the device's language and the headings of bands `h2`, by `GetSkillMap` with `ACTION_ADULT` and the open views from the shared cache (the block "Skill map" above), without choosing a skill, and "Back to the progress";
  - "Traps that repeat" — from the first page of `GetProgress` and only from it: `description` as the heading and `trap_advice` — "Advice", with the `lang` of the module's explanation language, the module, in how many closed attempts and the last time;
  - "Attempts" — newest first, by a page of `GetProgress`, and "Show more" by `next_page_token` with focus on the first new attempt, and for a page without attempts — on the last one shown or on the heading "Attempts". A failure of the first page is under this heading. An attempt has "Task {task}", the module, the lesson, the version, the result by the close reason and the wrong answers, the wrong answers, when it was begun and closed, "Why it was given" — or "Why its lesson was given" — with the words of the reason code and "Chosen", and without `chosen_at` — "The learner began it again: the program chose nothing". The submissions of an essay with the rubric's marks will take the place left for them, with T47.
- **How they are named:** a task — by the local ID from the full ID: the format has no names of tasks. A lesson — by its name `lesson_title`, with the `lang` of the module's explanation language, and when `api` no longer reads the package of the attempt's version and the name is empty, by the local ID. A solved attempt — closed by a correct answer, by showing the solution or by "Done" — has "Skills": the names `skill_titles` as a list in the screen's language, each with the `lang` of the module's explanation language; another attempt does not have them — the task's skills are closed while it can be begun again (R40; Д228 `spec`). A module — by the name from the manifest of the current version of the connected module, otherwise from the newest manifest of the cache, otherwise by the ID; the cache is read by the same `cardsOf` as the cards, and only for the modules that are not among the connected ones or whose name did not come.
- **Reason code** — the nine values of `ReasonCode` in words for the adult (R35; section 3.8), an unknown one by a general line. It is not on the learner's screens: `GetProgress` and the field `reason_code` are known only to `src/adults/ProgressPage.tsx` and `src/adults/progress.ts` (concepts 12 spec; R100 spec).
- **Code:** `src/adults/progress.ts` — the routes, the pages, the IDs of records, the result and the words of the codes; `src/adults/ProgressPage.tsx` — `ProgressPage` and `ProgressMap`; `src/subjects/ModuleCards.tsx` — the shared module card with one button, its list and the profile's connected modules, by which both the list of skill maps and the profile's modules are built; `mapTitle` and `MapHeading` in `src/skills/SkillMap.tsx`. Checked: in Node — the pages by the token, the routes, the IDs of records, the result of each close reason and of an unknown one, the words of the nine codes in `en` and `ru`, all different, and the general line of an unknown code, and a walk over the sources of `src/` — that `GetProgress`, `reason_code` and the words of the codes are only in the section's progress; in Chromium — the path from the profile page, the traps with the module's language, the attempts with the result, the time and the words of the code, "Show more" with focus — and for a page without attempts, — the lesson by its name and the skills of a solved attempt with the module's language, and a lesson without a name — the ID (since T53, R40), an unknown code and close reason, the traps of the second page not shown, a failure of the next and of the first page and a retry, an empty progress, a failure of the list of modules and of the map with a retry, the map in the adult's view by `ACTION_ADULT` and back, the words in `ru`, the pages without a profile; through `AccountGate` — the learner's screens in the profile's language — "My subjects", the task, "Something else" and the skill map — without a single text of a code and without `GetProgress`, and in the section — all nine; the accessibility checks — the progress page and the map in the adult's view with an open skill, in `en`, `ru` and the pseudo-language, at 1024 and 320 px.

**The questions of `spec` about the screens of `app`** (section 2) — what `app` does with them:
- **О-13, ranks and persona.** Until it is decided — a neutral tutor without ranks and a persona; the persona's voice will come with it (R07). Ranks on the skill map are also a detailing of `skill-map`.
- **О-7, showing the solution of a task** — closed by R82 spec: to a learner of the category `adult` the button is there from the start of the attempt, to a child from the fourth wrong answer, with the adult's setting "ask before showing" and the refusal "an adult is needed"; showing ends the attempt with the outcome "solution shown" (section 3.3). The adult's button — E2: in the first app an adult learns. To a child, the setting and the adult action behind a PIN — E4.
- **О-14, tariff and platform AI quota.** In the first slice platform AI is without payment, within a quota (product 10 spec): the quota is in dollars per family day, with caps of the profile and of the account, and the learner and the adult see its share, not the sum (О-14; concepts 14 spec). The day ends at midnight by the family's clock, so with requests to platform AI `app` sends the device's clock offset from UTC, in minutes (R118 spec; П-23 closed; concepts 15 spec). E2 — the offset in requests and the quota's words; since T49 (R37) the quota says when it comes back — "today", "tomorrow" or a date by the device's calendar, — and the block "Tutor" names the quota and the limit of platform AI both under the rung's fallback text and after a correct answer without words. The quota share is given since v0.16.3 by `GetQuota` (the 2026-11-12 amendment to R98 spec; Д229 `spec`), and since T53 `app` shows it (R40): to the learner as a line under the heading "My subjects", "Tutor time today: {percent} used", and at a cap reached by the words of the quota's refusal; to the adult as the section "Tutor time" on the profile page: the shares of the profile and of the account, that the cap is reached and when the quota renews. Without a percentage — the tariff has no caps — the learner sees no line, and the adult sees that platform AI is closed; from an `api` without `GetQuota` there is neither the line nor the section. Payment — stage 3.
- **О-5, the mode without AI.** The choice of an option is already checked without AI (architecture 4.2 spec). The whole mode will come when `spec` decides it and modules give tasks with input and choice.
- **О-21, a hint on request** — closed by R82 spec: before the first wrong answer — "givens and unknown" and the technique, after it — the words of the current rung once more, without AI, and "explain differently" — once per rung (section 3.3). E2.
- **О-23, presentation style.** `app` decided its part, and `spec` recorded it: voicing is in the presentation style (R07; О-23), and the default mode is a field of the profile of its own, not in the style (the 2026-11-02 amendment to R05). Neither goes into an AI call. The rest — with `tutor` and О-13.
- **П-20, what to write to a learner under 18** — closed by R127 spec: the reminder not to write names before each submission of a work that goes to AI whole, and the section "Help" for a learner under 18 — at `app`. The words of the reminder are by R10, and the screen is E3, with the essay module; the section "Help" — E4, with the first child.

**What each epic takes from the decisions.**

| Decision | E2 — the first app | E3 — the first slice | E4 — before the first child |
|---|---|---|---|
| R03 — framework and build | everything: the devcontainer, CI and the recipes (section 3.1) and the first release of the build | — | — |
| R04 — storage on the device | the profile's database, the cache, the registry, erasure and sign-out | essays and the page "This device" — the page since T38 (the 2026-11-12 amendment to R04) | export |
| R05 — solving steps | everything except the default mode in the section for adults: for a new profile it is "by steps" | the default mode — in the section for adults, since T37 (R29) | — |
| R06 — lesson player | everything except the label "sequential" | the label, with "My subjects" by modules — since T43 (R34) | — |
| R07 — voicing | — | everything | — |
| R08 — own key | — | everything except the PIN of the owner profile | the PIN of the owner profile and its closing period, when the account gets a child's profile |
| R09 — deployment configuration | `deployment.local.json`, the check of the file and both layers of the CSP in `vite preview` | deployment with `infra` | — |
| R10 — languages | the dictionaries `en` and `ru` from the first line; the device's language | the profile's language — in the section for adults, and the learner's screens in it, since T37 (R29); legal texts in English, with a line in the screen's language | — |
| R11 — accessibility | CI checks from the first screen | the page "Accessibility" | manual checks on devices (Д10 in table 4 of RUN.md); the closing period of the owner profile |

Before the first release of the build — immutable releases and the ruleset of the tags `v*` (Д54 in table 4 of RUN.md `spec`; R77 spec): the author set them up before T31, which turned publishing on (section 3.1; the 2026-11-12 amendment to R25). The tasks of E2 close the debts Д3 — T16, Д11 — T19, the CI checks of Д10 — T19, the test of the built page in Chromium of Д9 — T17, and of Д8 — T22, the measurement of the writing history by mouse of Д4 — T25. The devices — iPad, Android, WebKit and Firefox — are not available to the first app, which runs only on `127.0.0.1`, so the sign-in by the window of Д8 and the manual checks of Д9 wait for deployment in E3, as does voicing — Д5 and Д6. The closing of the owner profile — Д7 — and the manual accessibility checks — Д10 — wait for E4: both are needed when the account has a child (table 4 of RUN.md).

**What `spec` answered after v0.6.2**, `app` took with the move to v0.6.6 (T12; R12): the answers to the rows В4–В29 — two of them not as recommended, the 2026-11-02 amendments to R05 and R08, — R82 spec — showing the solution and a hint on request, R83 spec — contracts over Connect, R78 spec — icons, R79 spec — a stage with a browser in the template, R84 and R85 spec, П-23 and П-24.

**What `spec` answered after v0.6.6**, `app` took with the move to v0.13.0 (T13; R13): the answers to the rows В30–В42 — three of them not as recommended: legal texts only in English, a mark without a link to a phrase and the closing period of the owner profile without "do not close" (the 2026-11-10 amendments to R08 and R10), — and "no access" for an account in the device's registry (the 2026-11-10 amendment to R04). The rest that this tag brought — the contract `novatrail.api.v1` (R97–R100 spec), format 0.3 and 0.4, the part `deployment/`, the legal part R112–R131 spec and the questions it closed — the task T14 laid out: sections 1, 2, 3.1–3.8 and 3.10, the second 2026-11-10 amendments to R04 and R06 and table 2 of RUN.md.

## 4. Sources

Since T53, 2026-11-12, the records of sources — the external facts this document stands on, with the address, the date read and the quote — are in [sources.md](sources.md), and the results of measurements in [measurements.md](measurements.md) (RULES.md, The bar and How we work; R143 spec). The claims of sections 1–3 cite a record: `sources 3.1`, `measurements 2`. The section stays a pointer: the decision log and the archives of `docs/plan/` cite "section 4", and they are not rewritten.
